<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>ISB PP Sustainability — Synthetic Demonstration</title><style>
*{box-sizing:border-box}
html{scroll-behavior:smooth}
body{margin:0;background:#07141d;color:#e8f2f7;font:14px Arial,Helvetica,sans-serif}
header{padding:18px 22px 15px;border-bottom:1px solid #294352;background:#091923;position:sticky;top:0;z-index:20}
h1{margin:0 0 5px;font-size:25px}.sub{color:#a8bdc9}.tag{margin-top:5px;color:#64d4ff;font-weight:700}
.tabs{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(9,1fr);gap:6px;margin-top:14px}
.tab{background:#102632;color:#dceaf1;border:1px solid #365565;border-radius:9px;padding:10px 5px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px}
.tab.active{background:#147eb0;border-color:#55c9ff;color:#fff}
.wrap{padding:18px 22px;max-width:1500px;margin:auto}
.panel{display:none}.panel.active{display:block}
.card{background:#0b1c26;border:1px solid #294756;border-radius:13px;padding:17px;margin-bottom:14px}
h2{margin:0 0 7px;font-size:21px}h3{font-size:15px;margin:20px 0 9px}
.desc,.muted{color:#a8bdc9}
.metrics{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:10px;margin:13px 0}
.metric{background:#0d202b;border:1px solid #2b4b5b;border-radius:10px;padding:13px}
.metric small,.metric span{display:block;color:#91a9b6;font-size:10px}
.metric b{display:block;font-size:18px;margin:5px 0}
.flow{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:14px 0}
.node{background:#102936;border:1px solid #31576a;border-radius:10px;padding:11px 14px;text-align:center}
.node b,.node span{display:block}.node span{font-size:10px;color:#a8bdc9;margin-top:4px}.arrow{color:#55c9ff;font-size:18px}
.two{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:14px}.three{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:10px}
.box{background:#0d202b;border:1px solid #2b4b5b;border-radius:10px;padding:13px}
.box b{display:block;margin-bottom:6px}.box p{margin:5px 0;color:#b7cbd5;font-size:12px;line-height:1.45}
.conf{display:inline-block;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:12px;font-weight:700;font-size:11px}
.low{background:#5b2630;color:#ffc4ce}.med{background:#604b1d;color:#ffe2a0}.high{background:#174b3c;color:#9ff0cf}
.action{background:#27b3e7;border:0;border-radius:9px;color:white;font-weight:700;padding:11px 15px;cursor:pointer}
.log{margin-top:12px;background:#061018;border:1px solid #284453;border-radius:9px;padding:11px;min-height:92px;max-height:180px;overflow:auto;font:12px Consolas,monospace;color:#b9d9e8}
.tablewrap{overflow:auto}
.table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11px;margin-top:10px;background:#fff;color:#263b48}
.table th,.table td{border:1px solid #cdd9e0;padding:8px;text-align:left;vertical-align:top}
.table th{background:#eaf2f7;color:#225f9d}.table tr:nth-child(even) td{background:#f8fbfd}
.ribbon{background:#102936;border-left:4px solid #27b3e7;padding:11px 13px;border-radius:8px;margin:12px 0;color:#cce8f5}
.path{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin:10px 0}
.stage{padding:7px 9px;border:1px solid #365565;border-radius:16px;background:#102632;font-size:11px}
.stage.on{border-color:#55c9ff;color:#72d9ff}
.kpis{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:10px}
.kpi{background:#f7fbfd;border:1px solid #d6e2e8;border-radius:10px;padding:12px;color:#263b48}
.kpi small{display:block;color:#718492}.kpi b{font-size:20px}
.note{font-size:10px;color:#7e929d;margin-top:12px}
.ai{background:#eef8fc;color:#263b48;border-radius:12px;padding:14px;margin-top:12px}
.ai b{color:#1e6da8}.ai ul{margin:8px 0 0 18px;padding:0}.ai li{margin:6px 0}
.inner-menu,.graph-menu{display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:14px 0 9px}
.inner-btn,.graph-btn{background:#102632;color:#dceaf1;border:1px solid #365565;border-radius:18px;padding:8px 12px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px}
.inner-btn.active,.graph-btn.active{background:#147eb0;border-color:#55c9ff;color:#fff}
.inner-panel,.graph-panel{display:none}.inner-panel.active,.graph-panel.active{display:block}
.chart{background:#fff;color:#233746;border-radius:13px;padding:15px;border:1px solid #d9e3ea;margin-top:12px}
.chart-title{font-weight:700;color:#2572c7;margin-bottom:7px;font-size:14px}
.chart-sub{font-size:10px;color:#718492;margin-bottom:8px}
.bars .r{display:grid;grid-template-columns:180px 1fr 55px;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin:10px 0}
.track{height:18px;background:#e9eef2;border-radius:5px;overflow:hidden}
.fill{height:100%;background:#2d69c4}
.legend{display:flex;gap:13px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:10px;margin-top:7px;color:#5f7480}
.legend i{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;display:inline-block;margin-right:4px}
.donut{width:190px;height:190px;border-radius:50%;margin:15px auto;display:grid;place-items:center}
.donut>div{width:108px;height:108px;background:#fff;border-radius:50%;display:grid;place-content:center;text-align:center;color:#334}
.donut b{font-size:22px}
.supplier-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 28%,#cf9c2e 28% 75%,#2b9672 75% 100%)}
.supplier-dep-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 8%,#cf9c2e 8% 75%,#2b9672 75% 100%)}
.transport-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 10%,#cf9c2e 10% 80%,#2b9672 80% 100%)}
.transport-dep-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 10%,#cf9c2e 10% 80%,#2b9672 80% 100%)}
.plant-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 33%,#cf9c2e 33% 66%,#2b9672 66% 100%)}
.plant-dep-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 10%,#cf9c2e 10% 40%,#2b9672 40% 100%)}
.dist-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 25%,#cf9c2e 25% 75%,#2b9672 75% 100%)}
.dist-dep-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 20%,#cf9c2e 20% 70%,#2b9672 70% 100%)}
.customer-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 25%,#cf9c2e 25% 50%,#2b9672 50% 100%)}
.customer-dep-donut{background:conic-gradient(#b94151 0 20%,#cf9c2e 20% 65%,#2b9672 65% 100%)}
.ghg-donut{background:conic-gradient(#2d69c4 0 62%,#36c6d8 62% 84%,#79ddd0 84% 100%)}
.priority-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);gap:8px;margin-top:12px}
.priority-box{background:#f7fbfd;color:#263b48;border:1px solid #d6e2e8;border-radius:10px;padding:11px;text-align:center}
.priority-box b{display:block;font-size:18px;margin-top:4px}
.exposure{font-weight:700;color:#1e6da8}.why{font-size:10px;color:#617784}
.svg{width:100%;height:250px}.gridline{stroke:#d9e1e7;stroke-width:1}.axis{stroke:#7e8e99;stroke-width:1.2}
.blue{stroke:#286ac7;fill:none;stroke-width:4}.greenline{stroke:#22c89a;fill:none;stroke-width:4;stroke-dasharray:8 5}
.orange{stroke:#ff7a18;fill:none;stroke-width:4}.purple{stroke:#b54df0;fill:none;stroke-width:4}.yellow{stroke:#ffb000;fill:none;stroke-width:4;stroke-dasharray:8 5}
@media(max-width:1200px){.tabs{grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr)}}
@media(max-width:900px){.metrics,.kpis{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}.three,.two{grid-template-columns:1fr}.priority-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}}
@media(max-width:650px){header{position:static}.tabs,.metrics,.kpis{grid-template-columns:1fr}.bars .r{grid-template-columns:120px 1fr 45px}}

.barblue{fill:#2d69c4}.barcyan{fill:#39c3db}.barred{fill:#ee5b66}.barpink{fill:#f58aaa}
.status{display:inline-block;padding:4px 7px;border-radius:5px;background:#2d69c4;color:#fff}
.suggest{background:#f7fbfd;border:1px solid #d6e2e8;border-radius:10px;padding:14px;color:#263b48;line-height:1.55}


.entity-selector{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:14px 0}
.entity-btn{background:#102632;color:#dceaf1;border:1px solid #365565;border-radius:18px;padding:8px 13px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px}
.entity-btn.active{background:#147eb0;border-color:#55c9ff;color:#fff}
.entity-meta{font-size:11px;color:#9fb5c1;margin-top:4px}

select,input{background:#102936;color:#e8f2f7;border:1px solid #547384;border-radius:7px;padding:10px;margin:8px 8px 8px 0;max-width:100%}button:focus-visible,select:focus-visible{outline:3px solid #64d4ff} .details-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));gap:12px}.evidence-image{background:white;border-radius:10px;width:100%;height:auto}.field-list{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}.field-list label{padding:6px;border:1px solid #34566b;border-radius:6px}pre{white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;background:#061018;padding:15px} .chart .bars .r{grid-template-columns:160px 1fr 100px}.warning{border-left:4px solid #ffc65c;background:#30291c;padding:12px;margin-top:12px}.source-link{color:#64d4ff}.table td{overflow-wrap:anywhere;max-width:650px}details{margin:12px 0}summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:bold} .label{display:inline-block;padding:5px 9px;border:1px solid #8294a0;border-radius:8px;margin:5px}.record-meta{margin:10px 0;color:#b7cbd5}@media(max-width:750px){.details-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}header{position:static}.chart .bars .r{grid-template-columns:110px 1fr 70px}}</style></head><body><header>
<h1>PP Sustainability Data Digital Twin</h1>


<div class="tabs">
<button class="tab active" data-id="supplier">1 Supplier</button>
<button class="tab" data-id="transport">2 Transport</button>
<button class="tab" data-id="plant">3 PP Plant</button>
<button class="tab" data-id="distributor">4 Distributor</button>
<button class="tab" data-id="customer">5 Customer</button>
<button class="tab" data-id="circularity">6 End-of-Life</button>
<button class="tab" data-id="blockchain">7 Blockchain</button>
<button class="tab" data-id="dependency">8 Confidence & Dependency</button>
<button class="tab" data-id="holistic">9 Holistic ESG</button>
</div>
<div><label>Supply-chain filter <select id="chain"><option value="all">All three chains</option><option value="A">Chain A / Plant 1</option><option value="B">Chain B / Plant 2</option><option value="C">Chain C / Plant 3</option></select></label></div></header><main class="wrap"><section class="panel" id="supplier"></section><section class="panel" id="transport"></section><section class="panel" id="plant"></section><section class="panel" id="distributor"></section><section class="panel" id="customer"></section><section class="panel" id="circularity"></section><section "meaning": "scope1 plant kgco2e", "null_meaning": "Unknown or not applicable; never substitute zero without justification"}, {"table_name": "overview", "field_name": "scope2_plant_kgco2e", "type": "float", "unit": "kg CO2e", "meaning": "scope2 plant kgco2e", "null_meaning": "Unknown or not applicable; never substitute zero without justification"}, {"table_name": "overview", "field_name": "selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e", "type": "float", "unit": "kg CO2e", "meaning": "selected gate emissions kgco2e", "null_meaning": "Unknown or not applicable; never substitute zero without justification"}, {"table_name": "overview", "field_name": "all_modelled_transport_kgco2e", "type": "float", "unit": "kg CO2e", "meaning": "all modelled transport kgco2e", "null_meaning": "Unknown or not applicable; never substitute zero without justification"}, {"table_name": "overview", "field_name": "boundary_warning", "type": "str", "unit": "See field name / row metadata", "meaning": "boundary warning", "null_meaning": "Unknown or not applicable; never substitute zero without justification"}, {"table_name": "overview", "field_name": "status", "type": "str", "unit": "See field name / row metadata", "meaning": "status", "null_meaning": "Unknown or not applicable; never substitute zero without justification"}]};</script><script>const $=id=>document.getElementById(id), esc=v=>String(v??'Unknown / not applicable').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const fmt=(n,dp=2)=>Number(n).toLocaleString('en-IN',{maximumFractionDigits:dp}), sum=(rs,k)=>rs.reduce((a,r)=>a+(Number(r[k])||0),0);
const cfg={supplier:['suppliers','supplier_id','Supplier'],transport:['shipments','shipment_id','Shipment'],plant:['plants','plant_id','PP compounding plant'],distributor:['distributors','distributor_id','Distributor'],customer:['customers','customer_id','Industrial converter'],circularity:['recovery','recovery_id','Recovery record']};
let active='supplier',selection={},ledger=[],pending=false;
function chainOf(r){let id=r.destination_plant_id||r.plant_id||r.distributor_id||r.customer_id||r.transporter_id||r.recycler_id||r.entity_id||r.site_id;return id?.startsWith('P')?'ABC'[Number(id.slice(1))-1]:id?.slice(1,2)}
function rows(t){return DB[t].filter(r=>$('chain').value==='all'||chainOf(r)===$('chain').value)}
function badge(v){return '<span class="conf '+({High:'high',Medium:'med',Low:'low'}[v]||'med')+'">'+esc(v||'Unknown')+'</span>'}
function table(rs,fields){return '<div class="tablewrap"><table class="table"><thead><tr>'+fields.map(f=>'<th>'+esc(f.replaceAll('_',' '))+'</th>').join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+rs.map(r=>'<tr>'+fields.map(f=>'<td>'+esc(r[f])+'</td>').join('')+'</tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'}
function details(r){return '<details><summary>All fields and exact values</summary>'+table(Object.entries(r).map(([field,value])=>({field,value})),['field','value'])+'</details>'}
function bars(title,items,unit){let max=Math.max(...items.map(x=>x[1]),1);return '<div class="chart"><div class="chart-title">'+esc(title)+'</div><div class="chart-sub">Selected chain filter · '+esc(unit)+' · synthetic</div><div class="bars">'+items.map(([name,val])=>'<div class="r"><span>'+esc(name)+'</span><div class="track"><div class="fill" style="width:'+Math.max(0,val/max*100)+'%"></div></div><b>'+fmt(val)+'</b></div>').join('')+'</div></div>'}
function kpis(items){return '<div class="metrics">'+items.map(([label,v,sub])=>'<div class="metric"><small>'+esc(label)+'</small><b>'+esc(v)+'</b><span>'+esc(sub||'')+'</span></div>').join('')+'</div>'}
function note(){return '<div class="warning">All activity and emission-factor values are simulated. Confidence describes a fictional evidence scenario, not verified accuracy. Selected footprints exclude packaging, upstream fuel, ancillary waste treatment, use and final end-of-life.</div>'}
function invoice(s){return '<svg class="evidence-image" viewBox="0 0 620 380" role="img" aria-label="Generated sample invoice '+esc(s.invoice_number)+'"><rect width="620" height="380" fill="#fff"/><rect width="620" height="65" fill="#e6f2f7"/><text x="25" y="40" font-size="23" fill="#16415b">SAMPLE INVOICE — DEMONSTRATION ONLY</text><g font-family="Arial" font-size="16" fill="#243b4c"><text x="25" y="100">Invoice: '+esc(s.invoice_number)+'</text><text x="25" y="130">Date: '+esc(s.invoice_date)+' · Supplier: '+esc(s.supplier_id)+'</text><text x="25" y="160">Buyer: '+esc(s.destination_plant_id)+' · Batch: '+esc(s.supplier_batch_id)+'</text><text x="25" y="195">'+esc(s.material)+' — '+fmt(s.quantity_kg)+' kg</text><text x="25" y="225">Unit price: INR '+fmt(s.unit_price_inr_per_kg)+' / kg</text><text x="25" y="255">Amount: INR '+fmt(s.spend_inr)+' (tax excluded)</text><text x="25" y="300">Restricted · synthetic document · no real tax registration</text><text x="25" y="335">Illustrates quantity evidence; does not verify a carbon footprint.</text></g></svg>'}
function meter(r){let id=r.plant_id||r.distributor_id||r.customer_id;return '<svg class="evidence-image" viewBox="0 0 620 300" role="img" aria-label="Synthetic meter reading"><rect width="620" height="300" fill="#fff"/><text x="25" y="40" font-family="Arial" font-size="22" fill="#16415b">SAMPLE METER LOG — '+esc(id)+'</text><rect x="25" y="65" width="570" height="75" rx="8" fill="#152d38"/><text x="45" y="115" font-family="monospace" font-size="34" fill="#a4edc5">'+fmt(r.meter_end_kwh)+' kWh</text><g font-family="Arial" font-size="17" fill="#243b4c"><text x="25" y="175">Meter '+esc(r.meter_id)+'</text><text x="25" y="205">Start '+fmt(r.meter_start_kwh)+' → End '+fmt(r.meter_end_kwh)+'</text><text x="25" y="235">September usage: '+fmt(r.meter_end_kwh-r.meter_start_kwh)+' kWh</text><text x="25" y="270">Internal · generated sample · calibration not independently checked</text></g></svg>'}
function factor(id){let f=DB.emission_factors.find(x=>x.factor_id===id);return '<p><b>'+esc(id)+'</b>: '+f.value+' '+esc(f.unit)+' · '+esc(f.description)+'<br>Source: '+esc(f.source)+'<br>Boundary: '+esc(f.boundary)+'</p>'}
function calc(r,t){let body='';if(t==='supplier')body='<p>'+fmt(r.quantity_kg)+' kg × '+r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg+' kg CO₂e/kg = <b>'+fmt(r.quantity_kg*r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg)+' kg CO₂e</b></p>'+factor(r.pcf_factor_id);if(t==='transport')body='<p>('+fmt(r.cargo_kg)+' ÷ 1,000) tonnes × '+r.distance_km+' km × 0.10 = <b>'+fmt(r.emissions_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e</b></p>'+factor('EF06');if(t==='plant')body='<p>Electricity: '+r.electricity_kwh+' × '+DB.emission_factors.find(f=>f.factor_id==='EF04').value+' = '+fmt(r.scope2_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e<br>Diesel: '+r.diesel_litres+' × 2.68 = '+fmt(r.scope1_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e<br>Selected gate burden: materials '+fmt(r.raw_material_emissions_kgco2e)+' + inbound '+fmt(r.inbound_transport_emissions_kgco2e)+' + electricity + diesel = <b>'+fmt(r.selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e</b><br>Intensity: gate burden ÷ '+r.saleable_output_kg+' kg = '+fmt(r.selected_gate_intensity_kgco2e_per_kg,4)+' kg CO₂e/kg</p>'+factor('EF04')+factor('EF05');if(t==='distributor')body='<p>'+r.allocated_electricity_kwh+' allocated kWh × '+DB.emission_factors.find(f=>f.factor_id==='EF04').value+' = <b>'+fmt(r.storage_emissions_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e</b>. Entire electricity allocated to this single demo product.</p>'+factor('EF04');if(t==='customer')body='<p>Upstream allocation = (plant gate + plant-to-distributor + warehouse emissions) × 9,500/9,700 = '+fmt(r.selected_upstream_allocated_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e.<br>Finished burden adds delivery and '+r.electricity_kwh+' × '+DB.emission_factors.find(f=>f.factor_id==='EF04').value+' conversion electricity = <b>'+fmt(r.selected_finished_product_emissions_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e</b>.<br>Intensity = finished burden / '+r.finished_product_kg+' kg = '+fmt(r.selected_intensity_kgco2e_per_kg,4)+' kg CO₂e/kg.</p>'+factor('EF04');if(t==='circularity')body='<p>'+r.received_kg+' kg waste × 0.05 = <b>'+fmt(r.recycling_proxy_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e</b>. Electricity proxy only; waste transport and rejects treatment excluded. No avoided-emissions credit.</p>'+factor('EF07');return '<details open><summary>Carbon calculation basis — assumptions, not verified standard values</summary><div class="box">'+body+note()+'</div></details>'}
function renderEntity(t){let [tableName,id,title]=cfg[t],rs=rows(tableName);let key=selection[t];if(!rs.some(r=>r[id]===key))key=rs[0][id];selection[t]=key;let r=rs.find(r=>r[id]===key),html='<div class="card"><h2>'+title+'</h2><div class="desc">PP compounding and non-food plastics · detailed linked records</div><div class="entity-selector">'+rs.map(x=>'<button class="entity-btn '+(x[id]===key?'active':'')+'" data-select="'+esc(x[id])+'">'+esc(x[id])+'</button>').join('')+'</div>';
 let qty=r.quantity_kg??r.cargo_kg??r.input_kg??r.received_kg;let carbon=t==='supplier'?r.quantity_kg*r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg:r.emissions_kgco2e??r.selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e??r.storage_emissions_kgco2e??r.selected_finished_product_emissions_kgco2e??r.recycling_proxy_kgco2e;
 html+=kpis([['Record',key],['Quantity',fmt(qty)+' kg'],['Selected footprint',fmt(carbon)+' kg CO₂e',''],['Data confidence',r.confidence,'']]);html+='<div class="record-meta">'+badge(r.confidence)+' <span class="label">'+esc(r.confidentiality)+'</span> <span class="label">SIMULATED</span></div>';
 if(t==='supplier'){let e=DB.evidence.find(x=>x.evidence_id===r.evidence_id),total=sum(rows('suppliers'),'quantity_kg');html+='<div class="ribbon">Material: '+esc(r.material)+' · Quantity dependency: '+fmt(r.quantity_kg/total*100)+'% of selected purchases · Physical recycled content: '+r.physical_recycled_content_pct+'% · '+esc(r.confidence_reason)+'</div><div class="details-grid"><div>'+invoice(r)+'</div><div class="box"><h3>Evidence score</h3><p>Completeness '+e.completeness_pct+'; recency '+e.recency_score+'; method '+e.method_score+'; review '+e.review_score+'. Mean rounded = '+e.overall_score+'.</p><p>High ≥80; Medium 50–79; Low &lt;50. This is a project rubric, not a certification standard.</p><p>Invoice supports quantity. A product footprint additionally requires method, boundary, allocation and review. PCR certificate status: '+esc(r.certificate_status)+'</p></div></div>'}
 if(['plant','distributor','customer'].includes(t))html+='<div class="details-grid"><div>'+meter(r)+'</div><div class="box"><h3>Evidence and confidentiality</h3><p>Usage = closing − opening cumulative reading. Sample period: September 2026.</p><p>High: complete reconciled meter/production scenario; Medium: manual capture with partial review; Low: allocated/estimated evidence scenario. All actual values remain synthetic.</p><p>Internal metadata label does not restrict access in this static site.</p></div></div>';
 if(t==='plant'){html+=bars('Plant material balance',[['Saleable compound',r.saleable_output_kg],['Segregated scrap',r.segregated_scrap_kg],['Contained loss',r.contained_process_loss_kg]],'kg');html+=table(DB.batch_links.filter(x=>x.output_batch_id===r.output_batch_id),['input_batch_id','output_batch_id','input_quantity_kg','allocated_retained_kg'])}
 if(t==='transport')html+='<div class="ribbon">'+esc(r.leg_type)+' · '+esc(r.origin_id)+' → '+esc(r.destination_id)+' · '+esc(r.batch_id)+' · '+esc(r.vehicle_type)+'<br>'+esc(r.evidence_reference)+' · assumed distance; empty return excluded. No fuel-method total is added.</div>';
 if(t==='distributor')html+=bars('Stock reconciliation',[['Received',r.received_kg],['Dispatched',r.dispatched_kg],['Closing stock',r.closing_stock_kg]],'kg');
 if(t==='customer')html+=bars('Conversion balance',[['Consumed PP',r.consumed_kg],['Finished articles',r.finished_product_kg],['Scrap',r.scrap_kg]],'kg');
 if(t==='circularity')html+=bars('Recovery balance',[['Waste received',r.received_kg],['Recovered',r.recovered_kg],['Rejects',r.reject_kg]],'kg');
 let entityId=r.supplier_id||r.plant_id||r.distributor_id||r.customer_id||r.transporter_id||r.recycler_id; let entity=DB.entities.find(e=>e.entity_id===entityId); if(entity)html+='<details><summary>Organisation, site and contact fields</summary>'+table(Object.entries(entity).map(([field,value])=>({field,value})),['field','value'])+'</details>'; if(t==='plant'){let risk=DB.site_risks.find(x=>x.site_id===r.plant_id);html+='<details><summary>Location-risk scenario (invented, not map-derived)</summary>'+table([risk],['site_id','water_stress','flood_risk','deforestation_risk','risk_source'])+'</details>';} html+=calc(r,t)+details(r)+'<button class="action" data-log>Capture selected demonstration record</button><div class="log" id="'+t+'Log">Ready — no external submission.</div></div>';
 $(t).innerHTML=html;$(t).querySelectorAll('[data-select]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{selection[t]=b.dataset.select;renderEntity(t)});$(t).querySelector('[data-log]').onclick=()=>{$(t+'Log').textContent='Selected '+key+' | batch references checked | '+r.confidence+' evidence scenario | confidentiality '+r.confidentiality+' | '+fmt(carbon)+' kg CO₂e within displayed boundary. Captured locally for demonstration.'};
}
function groups(){return [['Suppliers',rows('suppliers'),'quantity_kg'],['Transporters',Object.values(rows('shipments').reduce((a,r)=>{let x=a[r.transporter_id]??={confidence:r.confidence,tonne_km:0};x.tonne_km+=r.cargo_kg/1000*r.distance_km;return a},{})),'tonne_km'],['Plants',rows('plants'),'saleable_output_kg'],['Distributors',rows('distributors'),'dispatched_kg'],['Converters',rows('customers'),'consumed_kg']]}
function renderConfidence(){let html='<div class="card"><h2>Confidence & dependency</h2><p>Evidence confidence and environmental performance are separate. High confidence does not mean low emissions. Factors remain assumed even for High records.</p>';for(let [name,rs,weight] of groups()){let total=sum(rs,weight);html+='<h3>'+name+' · dependency basis: '+esc(weight.replaceAll('_',' '))+'</h3><div class="two">'+bars('Entity count',['Low','Medium','High'].map(c=>[c,rs.filter(x=>x.confidence===c).length]),'entities')+bars('Quantity-weighted dependency',['Low','Medium','High'].map(c=>[c,sum(rs.filter(x=>x.confidence===c),weight)/total*100]),'%')+'</div>'}html+='<h3>Supplier evidence rubric</h3>'+table(rows('evidence').length?rows('evidence'):DB.evidence,['entity_id','record_type','completeness_pct','recency_score','method_score','review_score','overall_score','confidence'])+'</div>';$('dependency').innerHTML=html}
function ring(title,items,unit){const colors=['#ee697b','#edb94c','#34caa0','#3da9ee','#b198ff'];let total=items.reduce((s,x)=>s+x[1],0),at=0;let stops=items.map((x,i)=>{let start=at;at+=total?x[1]/total*100:0;return colors[i%colors.length]+' '+start+'% '+at+'%'}).join(',');return '<div class="chart"><div class="chart-title">'+esc(title)+'</div><div class="donut" style="background:conic-gradient('+stops+')"><div><b>'+fmt(total)+'</b><small>'+esc(unit)+'</small></div></div><div class="legend">'+items.map(([n,v],i)=>'<span><i style="background:'+colors[i%colors.length]+'"></i>'+esc(n)+': '+fmt(v)+' ('+fmt(total?v/total*100:0)+'%)</span>').join('')+'</div></div>'}
function comparison(title,rs,series,unit){let max=Math.max(1,...rs.flatMap(r=>series.map(s=>s[1](r))));return '<div class="chart"><div class="chart-title">'+esc(title)+'</div><div class="chart-sub">'+esc(unit)+' · September snapshot</div>'+rs.map(r=>'<h3>'+esc(r.plant_id||r.customer_id||r.distributor_id)+'</h3>'+series.map(([name,fn,color])=>'<div class="bars"><div class="r"><span>'+esc(name)+'</span><div class="track"><div class="fill" style="background:'+color+';width:'+fn(r)/max*100+'%"></div></div><b>'+fmt(fn(r))+'</b></div></div>').join('')).join('')+'</div>'}
function stageShares(){return [['Suppliers',rows('suppliers'),'quantity_kg','supplier_id'],['Transporters',Object.values(rows('shipments').reduce((a,r)=>{let x=a[r.transporter_id]??={transporter_id:r.transporter_id,confidence:r.confidence,tonne_km:0};x.tonne_km+=r.cargo_kg/1000*r.distance_km;return a},{})),'tonne_km','transporter_id'],['Plants',rows('plants'),'saleable_output_kg','plant_id'],['Distributors',rows('distributors'),'dispatched_kg','distributor_id'],['Customers',rows('customers'),'consumed_kg','customer_id']]}
function confidenceViews(weighted){return stageShares().map(([name,rs,weight,id])=>{let total=sum(rs,weight);return ring(name+' — '+(weighted?'dependency share':'confidence count'),['Low','Medium','High'].map(c=>[c,weighted?sum(rs.filter(r=>r.confidence===c),weight)/total*100:rs.filter(r=>r.confidence===c).length]),weighted?'%':'entities')}).join('')}
function renderESG(){let p=rows('plants'),c=rows('customers'),s=rows('suppliers'),tr=rows('shipments'),di=rows('distributors'),rec=rows('recovery');
const scope1=sum(p,'scope1_kgco2e'),scope2=sum(p,'scope2_kgco2e'),scope3=sum(p,'raw_material_emissions_kgco2e')+sum(p,'inbound_transport_emissions_kgco2e');
const views={
energy:['Energy Inputs','<div class="two">'+bars('Electricity by operating stage',[['Compounding plants',sum(p,'electricity_kwh')],['Distributor warehouses',sum(di,'warehouse_electricity_kwh')],['Customer conversion',sum(c,'electricity_kwh')]],'kWh')+ring('Plant electricity share',p.map(r=>[r.plant_id,r.electricity_kwh]),'kWh')+'</div>'+bars('Plant diesel use',p.map(r=>[r.plant_id,r.diesel_litres]),'litres')+'<p>Electricity and diesel are shown separately because kWh and litres cannot be added. Renewable electricity claim: 0% in this dataset.</p>'],
scope:['GHG Scope','<div class="two">'+ring('Selected plant-perspective emissions',[['Scope 1 — diesel',scope1],['Scope 2 — electricity',scope2],['Scope 3 — materials/inbound',scope3]],'kg CO₂e')+comparison('Scope comparison by plant',p,[['Scope 1',r=>r.scope1_kgco2e,'#3da9ee'],['Scope 2',r=>r.scope2_kgco2e,'#34caa0'],['Selected Scope 3',r=>r.raw_material_emissions_kgco2e+r.inbound_transport_emissions_kgco2e,'#ee697b']],'kg CO₂e')+'</div><p>Reporting viewpoint: each PP compounder. Scope 3 here includes purchased materials and inbound transport only. It is not a complete Scope 3 inventory. Distributor and customer emissions appear in the value-chain view below; no cumulative footprints are added together.</p>'+bars('Non-overlapping value-chain activities',[['Supplier materials',sum(p,'raw_material_emissions_kgco2e')],['Plant operations',scope1+scope2],['All shipment legs',sum(tr,'emissions_kgco2e')],['Warehouses',sum(di,'storage_emissions_kgco2e')],['Customer conversion',sum(c,'conversion_emissions_kgco2e')]],'kg CO₂e; includes current-stock burdens')],
production:['Production vs Scope','<div class="two">'+bars('Saleable compound output',p.map(r=>[r.plant_id,r.saleable_output_kg]),'kg')+comparison('Operational intensity per kg of compound',p,[['Scope 1 intensity',r=>r.scope1_kgco2e/r.saleable_output_kg,'#3da9ee'],['Scope 2 intensity',r=>r.scope2_kgco2e/r.saleable_output_kg,'#34caa0'],['Selected Scope 3 intensity',r=>(r.raw_material_emissions_kgco2e+r.inbound_transport_emissions_kgco2e)/r.saleable_output_kg,'#ee697b']],'kg CO₂e/kg')+'</div>'+table(p,['plant_id','saleable_output_kg','scope1_kgco2e','scope2_kgco2e','selected_gate_intensity_kgco2e_per_kg'])+'<p>This is a site comparison for one month. No historical trend has been fabricated.</p>'],
target:['Target Tracking','<p>Set a demonstration reduction target against this month’s selected plant-gate intensity. This is a planning scenario, not a published company target or verified forecast.</p><label>Illustrative reduction target (%) <input id="targetPct" type="number" min="0" max="90" step="1" value="10"></label><div id="targetCharts"></div>'],
visibility:['Data Entry Visibility','<div class="two">'+bars('Available records by stage',[['Suppliers',s.length],['Shipments',tr.length],['Plants',p.length],['Distributors',di.length],['Customers',c.length],['Recovery lots',rec.length]],'records')+bars('Null fields requiring interpretation',[['Supplier',s.reduce((n,r)=>n+Object.values(r).filter(x=>x===null).length,0)],['Transport',tr.reduce((n,r)=>n+Object.values(r).filter(x=>x===null).length,0)]],'fields')+'</div><p>Null means unknown or not applicable—not zero. A populated synthetic field is not verified evidence. Transport fuel is unknown because this model uses mass × distance. Supplier additives have no PP melt-flow test.</p>'+table(s.map(r=>({entity:r.supplier_id,invoice:r.invoice_number,batch:r.supplier_batch_id,capture:DB.evidence.find(e=>e.entity_id===r.supplier_id).record_type,confidence:r.confidence})),['entity','invoice','batch','capture','confidence'])],
water:['Water','<div class="two">'+comparison('Plant water balance',p,[['Withdrawal',r=>r.water_withdrawal_m3,'#3da9ee'],['Consumption',r=>r.water_consumption_m3,'#edb94c'],['Discharge',r=>r.water_discharge_m3,'#34caa0']],'m³')+bars('Customer water withdrawal',c.map(r=>[r.customer_id,r.water_withdrawal_m3]),'m³')+'</div><p>Withdrawal = consumption + discharge. Internal recirculation is separate and is not added to withdrawal. Supplier water data is unavailable. Location-risk categories remain invented screening examples.</p>'],
waste:['Waste & Circularity','<div class="two">'+bars('Material balance — final outcomes',[['Finished articles',sum(c,'finished_product_kg')],['Distributor stock',sum(di,'closing_stock_kg')],['Contained plant loss',sum(p,'contained_process_loss_kg')],['Recovered material',sum(rec,'recovered_kg')],['Recovery rejects',sum(rec,'reject_kg')]],'kg')+ring('Feedstock composition',[['Primary PP',sum(s.filter(r=>r.material==='Primary PP'),'quantity_kg')],['Recycled PP',sum(s.filter(r=>r.pcr_pct>0),'quantity_kg')],['Additive masterbatch',sum(s.filter(r=>r.material==='Additive masterbatch'),'quantity_kg')]],'kg')+'</div>'+comparison('Scrap and recovery by chain',p,[['Plant scrap',r=>r.segregated_scrap_kg,'#edb94c'],['Customer scrap',r=>c.find(x=>chainOf(x)===chainOf(r)).scrap_kg,'#ee697b'],['Recovered from both',r=>sum(rec.filter(x=>chainOf(x)===chainOf(r)),'recovered_kg'),'#34caa0']],'kg')+'<p>Scrap is an intermediate flow and is not added again to final outcomes. Recovery yield is 90% of received scrap; no avoided-emissions credit is deducted.</p>'],
confidence:['Data Confidence','<div class="two">'+confidenceViews(false)+'</div><p>High/Medium/Low describes the evidence scenario. Supplier score is the rounded mean of completeness, recency, method and review. High ≥80, Medium 50–79, Low &lt;50. Invoice availability alone does not validate a product footprint.</p>'],
exposure:['Dependency Exposure','<div class="two">'+confidenceViews(true)+'</div>'+stageShares().map(([name,rs,weight,id])=>'<h3>'+name+' — individual weightage</h3>'+bars('Share of '+weight.replaceAll('_',' '),rs.map(r=>[r[id],r[weight]/sum(rs,weight)*100]),'%')+table(rs.map(r=>({entity:r[id],confidence:r.confidence,dependency_pct:fmt(r[weight]/sum(rs,weight)*100),weighted_low_confidence_pct:r.confidence==='Low'?fmt(r[weight]/sum(rs,weight)*100):0})),['entity','confidence','dependency_pct','weighted_low_confidence_pct'])).join('')+'<p>Shares total 100% within each stage and current filter. Supplier share uses purchased kg; transporter share uses tonne-km; plant share uses saleable kg; distributor/customer share uses dispatched/consumed kg. Do not average these into one risk score without an explicit policy.</p>']};
$('holistic').innerHTML='<div class="card"><h2>Holistic ESG — complete management view</h2>'+kpis([['Input',fmt(sum(p,'input_kg'))+' kg'],['Finished articles',fmt(sum(c,'finished_product_kg'))+' kg'],['Recycled input',fmt(sum(s.filter(r=>r.pcr_pct>0),'quantity_kg')/sum(s,'quantity_kg')*100)+'%'],['Recovered scrap',fmt(sum(rec,'recovered_kg'))+' kg']])+'<div class="graph-menu">'+Object.entries(views).map(([id,v],i)=>'<button class="graph-btn '+(!i?'active':'')+'" data-esg="'+id+'">'+v[0]+'</button>').join('')+'</div>'+Object.entries(views).map(([id,v],i)=>'<div class="graph-panel '+(!i?'active':'')+'" id="esg-'+id+'"><h3>'+v[0]+'</h3>'+v[1]+'</div>').join('')+'<details><summary>Social, governance and source references</summary><p>Safety incidents: '+sum(p,'safety_incidents')+' (synthetic). Training, wages and labour audits are unavailable. Confidentiality labels do not enforce access controls.</p>'+table(rows('governance_actions'),['entity_id','issue','priority','status','due_date'])+table(DB.emission_factors,['factor_id','value','unit','boundary','source'])+DB.references.map(r=>'<p><a class="source-link" href="'+esc(r.url)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">'+esc(r.title)+'</a> — '+esc(r.use_in_dataset)+'</p>').join('')+'</details><details><summary>Model boundaries</summary>'+note()+'</details></div>';
$('holistic').querySelectorAll('[data-esg]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{$('holistic').querySelectorAll('.graph-btn').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===b));$('holistic').querySelectorAll('.graph-panel').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x.id==='esg-'+b.dataset.esg))});
function target(){let pct=Math.min(90,Math.max(0,Number($('targetPct').value)||0));let items=p.map(r=>({plant_id:r.plant_id,baseline:r.selected_gate_intensity_kgco2e_per_kg,target:r.selected_gate_intensity_kgco2e_per_kg*(1-pct/100)}));$('targetCharts').innerHTML=comparison('Baseline and chosen target intensity',items,[['September baseline',r=>r.baseline,'#b198ff'],['Planning target',r=>r.target,'#34caa0']],'kg CO₂e/kg')+'<p>Target = baseline × (1 − '+pct+'/100). Actual post-intervention data: unavailable. Targets are not claimed as achieved reductions.</p>'}if($('targetPct')){$('targetPct').oninput=target;target()}
}

const important={supplier:['supplier_id','supplier_batch_id','quantity_kg','invoice_number','pcf_factor_id','physical_recycled_content_pct','confidence','confidentiality'],transport:['shipment_id','batch_id','origin_id','destination_id','cargo_kg','distance_km','factor_id','emissions_kgco2e','confidentiality'],plant:['plant_id','output_batch_id','input_kg','saleable_output_kg','meter_id','meter_end_kwh','selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e','confidence','confidentiality'],distributor:['distributor_id','batch_id','received_kg','dispatched_kg','closing_stock_kg','confidentiality'],customer:['customer_id','input_batch_id','finished_product_batch_id','consumed_kg','finished_product_kg','scrap_kg','confidentiality'],circularity:['recovery_id','source_batch_id','recycler_id','received_kg','recovered_kg','certificate_id','confidentiality']};
async function digest(v){if(!globalThis.crypto?.subtle)throw Error('SHA-256 requires a supported browser/secure context. Open on HTTPS or localhost.');let b=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',new TextEncoder().encode(typeof v==='string'?v:JSON.stringify(v)));return Array.from(new Uint8Array(b),x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('')}
function renderChain(){let html='<div class="card"><h2>Blockchain field selection & tamper detection</h2><p>Local SHA-256 hash-chain demonstration. No distributed consensus, identity verification or permanent storage. Hashing proves consistency, not truth. Restricted records are stored as hashes; original documents remain outside the ledger.</p><label>Stage <select id="stage">'+Object.keys(cfg).map(t=>'<option>'+t+'</option>').join('')+'</select></label><label>Record <select id="proof-record"></select></label><h3>Select fields to protect</h3><div class="field-list" id="proof-fields"></div><p class="muted">Evidence illustration hash is added for invoices/meters. Other document references are hashed as references, not as nonexistent file bytes.</p><button class="action" id="append">Append selected proof</button> <button class="action" id="verify">Verify chain</button> <button class="action" id="tamper">Demonstrate tampering</button> <button class="action" id="ledger-export">Export ledger</button><div class="log" id="chainLog">Ready.</div><div id="blocks"></div></div>';$('blockchain').innerHTML=html;let stage=$('stage');function options(){let [t,id]=cfg[stage.value];$('proof-record').innerHTML=rows(t).map(r=>'<option>'+esc(r[id])+'</option>').join('');$('proof-fields').innerHTML=important[stage.value].map(f=>'<label><input type="checkbox" checked value="'+f+'">'+esc(f)+'</label>').join('')}stage.onchange=options;options();$('append').onclick=async()=>{if(pending)return;pending=true;try{let [t,id]=cfg[stage.value],r=rows(t).find(x=>x[id]===$('proof-record').value),payload={};document.querySelectorAll('#proof-fields input:checked').forEach(x=>payload[x.value]=r[x.value]??null);if(!Object.keys(payload).length)throw Error('Select at least one field');let evidence=stage.value==='supplier'?invoice(r):r.meter_id?meter(r):r.evidence_reference||r.certificate_id||'No evidence bytes';let block={index:ledger.length,timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),stage:stage.value,record_id:r[id],previous_hash:ledger.at(-1)?.hash||'GENESIS',payload_hash:await digest(payload),evidence_hash:await digest(evidence),selected_fields:Object.keys(payload),confidentiality:r.confidentiality||'Internal'};block.hash=await digest(block);ledger.push(block);$('chainLog').textContent='Appended '+r[id]+'. Only hashes/metadata stored; values and image not in ledger.';showBlocks()}catch(e){$('chainLog').textContent=e.message}finally{pending=false}};$('verify').onclick=async()=>{try{for(let i=0;i<ledger.length;i++){let {hash,...b}=ledger[i];if(hash!==await digest(b)||b.previous_hash!==(ledger[i-1]?.hash||'GENESIS'))throw Error('Tampering detected at block '+i)}$('chainLog').textContent=ledger.length?'Chain consistent: '+ledger.length+' blocks. Source accuracy is not verified.':'No blocks yet.'}catch(e){$('chainLog').textContent=e.message}};$('tamper').onclick=()=>{if(!ledger.length){$('chainLog').textContent='Append a block first.';return}ledger[0].record_id+='-ALTERED';showBlocks();$('chainLog').textContent='First block altered. Click Verify chain to detect the change.'};$('ledger-export').onclick=()=>download(ledger,'demo_hash_chain.json');showBlocks()}
function showBlocks(){if($('blocks'))$('blocks').innerHTML=ledger.map(b=>'<details><summary>Block '+b.index+' · '+esc(b.record_id)+'</summary><pre>'+esc(JSON.stringify(b,null,2))+'</pre></details>').join('')}
function download(data,name){let url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify(data,null,2)],{type:'application/json'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000)}
function renderAll(){Object.keys(cfg).forEach(renderEntity);renderConfidence();renderESG();renderChain();document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p=>p.classList.toggle('active',p.id===active))}
document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{active=b.dataset.id;document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===b));document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x.id===active));window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'})});$('chain').onchange=renderAll;renderAll();

const baseline=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DB));
const ef=id=>DB.emission_factors.find(f=>f.factor_id===id).value;
function recalculate(){
 for(const s of DB.shipments)s.emissions_kgco2e=s.cargo_kg/1000*s.distance_km*ef(s.factor_id);
 for(const p of DB.plants){p.scope1_kgco2e=p.diesel_litres*ef('EF05');p.scope2_kgco2e=p.electricity_kwh*ef('EF04');p.meter_end_kwh=p.meter_start_kwh+p.electricity_kwh;p.raw_material_emissions_kgco2e=DB.suppliers.filter(s=>s.destination_plant_id===p.plant_id).reduce((a,s)=>a+s.quantity_kg*s.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg,0);p.inbound_transport_emissions_kgco2e=sum(DB.shipments.filter(s=>s.destination_id===p.plant_id),'emissions_kgco2e');p.selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e=p.scope1_kgco2e+p.scope2_kgco2e+p.raw_material_emissions_kgco2e+p.inbound_transport_emissions_kgco2e;p.selected_gate_intensity_kgco2e_per_kg=p.selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e/p.saleable_output_kg;}
 for(const d of DB.distributors){d.storage_emissions_kgco2e=d.allocated_electricity_kwh*ef('EF04');d.meter_end_kwh=d.meter_start_kwh+d.warehouse_electricity_kwh;}
 for(const c of DB.customers){c.meter_end_kwh=c.meter_start_kwh+c.electricity_kwh;let plant=DB.plants.find(p=>p.output_batch_id===c.input_batch_id),dist=DB.distributors.find(d=>d.customer_id===c.customer_id),pd=DB.shipments.find(s=>s.origin_id===plant.plant_id&&s.destination_id===dist.distributor_id),dc=DB.shipments.find(s=>s.destination_id===c.customer_id);c.conversion_emissions_kgco2e=c.electricity_kwh*ef('EF04');c.selected_upstream_allocated_kgco2e=(plant.selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e+pd.emissions_kgco2e+dist.storage_emissions_kgco2e)*c.consumed_kg/plant.saleable_output_kg;c.selected_finished_product_emissions_kgco2e=c.selected_upstream_allocated_kgco2e+dc.emissions_kgco2e+c.conversion_emissions_kgco2e;c.selected_intensity_kgco2e_per_kg=c.selected_finished_product_emissions_kgco2e/c.finished_product_kg;}
 const o=DB.overview[0];o.recovered_scrap_kg=sum(DB.recovery,'recovered_kg');o.scope1_plant_kgco2e=sum(DB.plants,'scope1_kgco2e');o.scope2_plant_kgco2e=sum(DB.plants,'scope2_kgco2e');o.selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e=sum(DB.plants,'selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e');o.all_modelled_transport_kgco2e=sum(DB.shipments,'emissions_kgco2e');
}

function compactCalc(r,t){let text='',f=ef('EF04');if(t==='supplier')text=fmt(r.quantity_kg)+' kg × '+r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg+' kg CO₂e/kg = '+fmt(r.quantity_kg*r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg)+' kg CO₂e';if(t==='transport')text=fmt(r.cargo_kg/1000)+' t × '+r.distance_km+' km × '+ef('EF06')+' = '+fmt(r.emissions_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e';if(t==='plant')text='Electricity: '+r.electricity_kwh+' kWh × '+f+' = '+fmt(r.scope2_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e. Diesel: '+r.diesel_litres+' L × '+ef('EF05')+' = '+fmt(r.scope1_kgco2e)+'. Selected gate total: '+fmt(r.selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e; intensity '+fmt(r.selected_gate_intensity_kgco2e_per_kg,4)+' kg CO₂e/kg.';if(t==='distributor')text=r.allocated_electricity_kwh+' kWh × '+f+' = '+fmt(r.storage_emissions_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e';if(t==='customer')text='Conversion electricity: '+r.electricity_kwh+' kWh × '+f+' = '+fmt(r.conversion_emissions_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e. Selected upstream + delivery + conversion = '+fmt(r.selected_finished_product_emissions_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e.';if(t==='circularity')text=r.received_kg+' kg × '+ef('EF07')+' = '+fmt(r.recycling_proxy_kgco2e)+' kg CO₂e (recycling proxy).';return '<details><summary>Calculation</summary><p>'+esc(text)+'</p><small>Assumed factors · partial footprint · synthetic records</small></details>'}
calc=compactCalc;
const renderOriginal=renderEntity;
renderEntity=function(t){renderOriginal(t);let r=DB[cfg[t][0]].find(r=>r[cfg[t][1]]===selection[t]),controls='',editable=true;
if(t==='plant')controls='<label>Electricity (kWh) <input id="edit-plant-kwh" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="'+r.electricity_kwh+'"></label><label>Electricity factor (kg CO₂e/kWh) <input id="edit-plant-factor" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="'+ef('EF04')+'"></label>';
if(t==='distributor')controls='<label>Warehouse electricity (kWh) <input id="edit-distributor-kwh" type="number" min="0" value="'+r.warehouse_electricity_kwh+'"></label>';
if(t==='customer')controls='<label>Conversion electricity (kWh) <input id="edit-customer-kwh" type="number" min="0" value="'+r.electricity_kwh+'"></label>';
if(t==='circularity')controls='<label>Recovery yield (%) <input id="edit-circularity-yield" type="number" min="0" max="100" step="0.1" value="'+r.recovery_yield_pct+'"></label>';
if(t==='transport')controls='<label>Distance (km) <input id="edit-transport-distance" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="'+r.distance_km+'"></label>';
if(t==='supplier')controls='<label>Material carbon factor (kg CO₂e/kg) <input id="edit-supplier-pcf" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="'+r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg+'"></label><label>Evidence <select id="edit-supplier-confidence"><option value="Low" '+(r.confidence==='Low'?'selected':'')+'>Estimate only · Low</option><option value="Medium" '+(r.confidence==='Medium'?'selected':'')+'>Invoice / manual allocation · Medium</option><option value="High" '+(r.confidence==='High'?'selected':'')+'>Complete reviewed evidence · High</option></select></label>';
let host=document.createElement('div');host.className='box';host.innerHTML=controls+(editable?'<button class="action" id="apply-'+t+'">Apply changes</button> ':'')+'<button class="action" id="reset-'+t+'">Reset original data</button><div id="edit-status-'+t+'" aria-live="polite"></div>';
$(t).querySelector('.metrics').insertAdjacentElement('afterend',host);
if(t==='supplier')$('edit-supplier-confidence').value=r.confidence;
function number(id){let raw=$(id).value,n=Number(raw);if(raw===''||!Number.isFinite(n)||n<0)throw Error('Enter a non-negative number.');return n}
if(editable)$('apply-'+t).onclick=()=>{try{let before=t==='plant'?r.scope2_kgco2e:t==='transport'?r.emissions_kgco2e:t==='supplier'?r.quantity_kg*r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg:t==='distributor'?r.storage_emissions_kgco2e:t==='customer'?r.conversion_emissions_kgco2e:r.recovered_kg;if(t==='distributor'){let n=number('edit-distributor-kwh');r.warehouse_electricity_kwh=n;r.allocated_electricity_kwh=n;}if(t==='customer')r.electricity_kwh=number('edit-customer-kwh');if(t==='circularity'){let n=number('edit-circularity-yield');if(n>100)throw Error('Recovery yield must be between 0 and 100%.');r.recovery_yield_pct=n;r.recovered_kg=r.received_kg*n/100;r.reject_kg=r.received_kg-r.recovered_kg;let batch=DB.batches.find(b=>b.batch_id===r.next_batch_id);if(batch)batch.quantity_kg=r.recovered_kg;let link=DB.batch_links.find(l=>l.output_batch_id===r.next_batch_id);if(link){link.retained_fraction=n/100;link.allocated_retained_kg=r.recovered_kg;}}if(t==='plant'){let k=number('edit-plant-kwh'),f=number('edit-plant-factor');r.electricity_kwh=k;let factor=DB.emission_factors.find(f=>f.factor_id==='EF04');factor.value=f;factor.source='User-edited assumption';}if(t==='transport'){r.distance_km=number('edit-transport-distance');r.distance_method='User-edited distance';}if(t==='supplier'){r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg=number('edit-supplier-pcf');r.pcf_factor_id='EF-'+r.supplier_id;let factor=DB.emission_factors.find(x=>x.factor_id===r.pcf_factor_id);if(!factor){factor={...DB.emission_factors.find(x=>x.factor_id==='EF01'),factor_id:r.pcf_factor_id};DB.emission_factors.push(factor)}factor.value=r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg;factor.description='Selected material footprint assumption';factor.source='User-edited assumption';r.confidence=$('edit-supplier-confidence').value;let e=DB.evidence.find(x=>x.evidence_id===r.evidence_id),v={High:[100,100,90,90],Medium:[80,80,60,50],Low:[50,60,30,0]}[r.confidence];[e.completeness_pct,e.recency_score,e.method_score,e.review_score]=v;e.overall_score=Math.round(v.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/4);e.confidence=r.confidence;e.record_type={Low:'Estimate only',Medium:'Invoice/manual allocation',High:'Complete reviewed evidence'}[r.confidence];r.confidence_reason=e.record_type+' (sample)';}
recalculate();renderAll();let after=t==='plant'?r.scope2_kgco2e:t==='transport'?r.emissions_kgco2e:t==='supplier'?r.quantity_kg*r.pcf_kgco2e_per_kg:t==='distributor'?r.storage_emissions_kgco2e:t==='customer'?r.conversion_emissions_kgco2e:r.recovered_kg;$('edit-status-'+t).textContent='Updated: '+fmt(before)+' → '+fmt(after)+(t==='circularity'?' kg recovered. Graphs refreshed.':' kg CO₂e. Graphs refreshed.');}catch(e){$('edit-status-'+t).textContent=e.message}};
$('reset-'+t).onclick=()=>{for(const k of Object.keys(DB))DB[k]=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(baseline[k]));renderAll();$('edit-status-'+t).textContent='Original data restored.'};
// Keep records, pictures and concise formula disclosure; remove long tutorial prose.
$(t).querySelectorAll('.desc,.warning,.metric span,.metric small + b + span,.record-meta .label:last-child').forEach(x=>x.remove());
let box=$(t).querySelector('.details-grid .box');if(box)box.innerHTML=t==='supplier'?'<h3>Evidence</h3><p>'+esc(r.confidence_reason)+'</p><p>Invoice '+esc(r.invoice_number)+' · '+esc(r.confidentiality)+'</p>':'<h3>Meter record</h3><p>'+esc(r.meter_id)+' · '+fmt(r.meter_start_kwh)+' → '+fmt(r.meter_end_kwh)+' kWh</p>';
};
const renderESGOriginal=renderESG;renderESG=function(){renderESGOriginal();$('holistic').querySelectorAll('p').forEach(x=>x.remove())};
const renderConfidenceOriginal=renderConfidence;renderConfidence=function(){renderConfidenceOriginal();$('dependency').querySelectorAll('p').forEach(x=>x.remove())};
const renderChainOriginal=renderChain;renderChain=function(){renderChainOriginal();$('blockchain').querySelectorAll('p').forEach(x=>x.remove())};
function switchTab(id){active=id;document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x.dataset.id===id));document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(x=>{x.classList.toggle('active',x.id===id);x.hidden=x.id!==id});window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'})}
document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>switchTab(b.dataset.id));
const renderAllOriginal=renderAll;renderAll=function(){renderAllOriginal();switchTab(active)};
renderAll();
</script><style id="passport-styles">.passport-card{margin:18px 0}#passport-dialog{background:#0b1c26;color:#e8f2f7;border:1px solid #55c9ff;border-radius:14px;width:min(1150px,94vw);max-height:90vh;padding:22px}#passport-dialog::backdrop{background:#000b}#passport-dialog .table{font-size:12px}#passport-dialog img{max-width:100%}@media(max-width:600px){#passport-dialog{padding:12px}#passport-dialog .metrics{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}}</style><script id="passport-addon">const PASSPORT_QR={"OUT-A":
