// Additive product passport module; original V5 code and records are unchanged.
const passportId=batch=>'PP-PASS-'+batch;
function passportContext(batch){let c=DB.customers.find(x=>x.finished_product_batch_id===batch||x.input_batch_id===batch);if(!c)return null;let p=DB.plants.find(x=>x.output_batch_id===c.input_batch_id),d=DB.distributors.find(x=>x.customer_id===c.customer_id),sup=DB.suppliers.filter(x=>x.destination_plant_id===p.plant_id),ship=DB.shipments.filter(x=>x.batch_id===p.output_batch_id||sup.some(s=>s.supplier_batch_id===x.batch_id)),recovery=DB.recovery.filter(x=>x.source_batch_id===p.output_batch_id||x.source_batch_id===c.finished_product_batch_id);return {c,p,d,sup,ship,recovery,batch,finished:batch===c.finished_product_batch_id}}
function passportButton(batch,label){return '<button class="action" data-passport="'+esc(batch)+'">'+esc(label||'View Product Passport')+'</button>'}
function bindPassports(host){host.querySelectorAll('[data-passport]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openPassport(b.dataset.passport));}
function passportHtml(batch){let x=passportContext(batch);if(!x)return '<p>Passport not found.</p>';let {p,c,d,sup,ship,recovery,finished}=x;let id=passportId(batch),parent=finished?passportId(p.output_batch_id):'Source material batches';let foot=finished?c.selected_finished_product_emissions_kgco2e:p.selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e;let quantity=finished?c.finished_product_kg:p.saleable_output_kg;
return '<h2>Product Sustainability Passport</h2><div class="record-meta">'+esc(id)+' · '+esc(batch)+' · Internal</div>'+kpis([['Product',finished?'Non-food PP article':'PP compound'],['Quantity',fmt(quantity)+' kg'],['Physical recycled content',fmt(c.physical_recycled_content_pct)+'%'],['Recorded footprint',fmt(foot)+' kg CO₂e']])+'<p><b>Parent:</b> '+esc(parent)+' '+(finished?passportButton(p.output_batch_id,'View source compound passport'):'')+'</p><div class="two"><div class="box"><h3>Recorded journey</h3><div class="flow">'+[sup.map(s=>s.supplier_id).join(', '),p.plant_id,d.distributor_id,c.customer_id].map(n=>'<span class="node">'+esc(n)+'</span>').join('<span class="arrow">→</span>')+'</div><p><b>Last recorded product event:</b> '+(finished?'Conversion into finished articles, 24 September 2026.':'Dispatch to distributor; linked conversion into finished articles.')+'</p><p><b>Finished-product use and final disposal:</b> Not yet recorded.</p><p><b>Scrap recovery:</b> Recorded separately; it does not prove recovery of the finished product.</p></div><div class="box"><h3>Passport QR</h3><img style="width:180px;height:180px;background:white" alt="QR for '+esc(id)+'" src="'+PASSPORT_QR[batch]+'"><p><a class="source-link" href="https://nimisha-raina.github.io/ISB/#passport='+encodeURIComponent(batch)+'">Hosted passport link</a></p><small>QR opens this passport after the updated HTML is published at your ISB website.</small></div></div><h3>Material origin & evidence confidence</h3>'+table(sup,['supplier_id','supplier_batch_id','material','quantity_kg','physical_recycled_content_pct','invoice_number','confidence'])+'<h3>Shipment history</h3>'+table(ship,['shipment_id','origin_id','destination_id','dispatch_date','cargo_kg','distance_km','emissions_kgco2e'])+'<h3>Production & conversion links</h3>'+table(DB.batch_links.filter(l=>l.output_batch_id===p.output_batch_id||l.output_batch_id===c.finished_product_batch_id),['input_batch_id','output_batch_id','input_quantity_kg','transformation','allocated_retained_kg'])+'<h3>Evidence references</h3>'+table([{entity:p.plant_id,reference:p.meter_id,confidence:p.confidence},{entity:d.distributor_id,reference:d.meter_id,confidence:d.confidence},{entity:c.customer_id,reference:c.meter_id,confidence:c.confidence}],['entity','reference','confidence'])+'<h3>Production and conversion scrap recovery</h3>'+table(recovery,['recovery_id','source_batch_id','received_kg','recovered_kg','reject_kg','certificate_id'])+'<details><summary>Sustainability figures & calculation basis</summary>'+table([{stage:p.plant_id,electricity_kwh:p.electricity_kwh,water_withdrawal_m3:p.water_withdrawal_m3},{stage:d.distributor_id,electricity_kwh:d.warehouse_electricity_kwh,water_withdrawal_m3:d.water_m3},{stage:c.customer_id,electricity_kwh:c.electricity_kwh,water_withdrawal_m3:c.water_withdrawal_m3}],['stage','electricity_kwh','water_withdrawal_m3'])+'<p>Selected footprint intensity: '+fmt(foot/quantity,4)+' kg CO₂e/kg. Cumulative footprints overlap and must not be added. Physical recycled share assumes proportional mixing and losses; PCR evidence is pending.</p>'+table(DB.emission_factors,['factor_id','value','unit','boundary','source'])+'<p>All passport records use the dashboard’s illustrative dataset. Missing lifecycle events remain unknown.</p></details>'}
function openPassport(batch){let modal=$('passport-dialog');if(!modal){modal=document.createElement('dialog');modal.id='passport-dialog';document.body.appendChild(modal)}modal.innerHTML='<button class="action" id="passport-close" style="float:right">Close</button><div id="passport-body">'+passportHtml(batch)+'</div>';bindPassports(modal);$('passport-close').onclick=()=>modal.close();if(!modal.open)modal.showModal();}
function linkedBatch(t,r){if(t==='supplier')return DB.plants.find(p=>p.plant_id===r.destination_plant_id)?.output_batch_id;if(t==='plant')return r.output_batch_id;if(t==='customer')return r.finished_product_batch_id;if(t==='circularity')return r.source_batch_id;let b=r.batch_id;if(b?.startsWith('IN-'))return DB.batch_links.find(l=>l.input_batch_id===b)?.output_batch_id;return b;}
const passportBaseEntity=renderEntity;
renderEntity=function(t){passportBaseEntity(t);let r=DB[cfg[t][0]].find(x=>x[cfg[t][1]]===selection[t]),batch=linkedBatch(t,r);if(!batch)return;let card=document.createElement('div');card.className='box passport-card';let text={supplier:'Material origin and supplier batch linked to the plant output passport.',transport:'Shipment and custody events linked to the product batch.',plant:'Output batch passport links all contributing material batches.',distributor:'Receipt and dispatch events retained in the batch history.',customer:'Finished-product passport linked to its source PP compound.',circularity:'Recovery event linked to its source batch; this record covers production scrap.'}[t];card.innerHTML='<h3>Product Sustainability Passport</h3><p>'+esc(passportId(batch))+' · '+esc(text)+'</p>'+passportButton(batch);let chart=$(t).querySelector('.chart');if(t==='customer'&&chart)chart.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',card);else $(t).querySelector('.card').appendChild(card);bindPassports(card)};
const passportBaseESG=renderESG;
renderESG=function(){passportBaseESG();let customers=rows('customers'),total=customers.length,linked=customers.filter(c=>passportContext(c.finished_product_batch_id)).length,card=document.createElement('div');card.className='box passport-card';card.innerHTML='<h3>Product Passport Coverage</h3>'+kpis([['Finished-batch passports',linked+' / '+total],['Linked upstream history',fmt(total?linked/total*100:0)+'%'],['Finished-product end-of-life','0% recorded'],['Missing final-use outcomes',total]])+'<p>Coverage measures linked records, not verification. Scrap recovery is distinct from finished-product end-of-life.</p>'+table(customers.map(c=>({passport_id:passportId(c.finished_product_batch_id),batch:c.finished_product_batch_id,source_passport:passportId(c.input_batch_id),customer:c.customer_id,confidence:c.confidence,final_product_end_of_life:'Not yet recorded'})),['passport_id','batch','source_passport','customer','confidence','final_product_end_of_life']);$('holistic').querySelector('.card').appendChild(card)};
const passportBaseChain=renderChain;
renderChain=function(){passportBaseChain();let host=document.createElement('div');host.className='box passport-card';host.innerHTML='<h3>Passport integrity proof</h3><select id="passport-proof-batch">'+rows('customers').map(c=>'<option value="'+c.finished_product_batch_id+'">'+passportId(c.finished_product_batch_id)+'</option>').join('')+'</select> <button class="action" id="passport-proof-add">Add passport snapshot hash</button><div id="passport-proof-status"></div>';$('blockchain').querySelector('.card').appendChild(host);$('passport-proof-add').onclick=async()=>{if(pending)return;pending=true;try{let batch=$('passport-proof-batch').value,x=passportContext(batch);let block={index:ledger.length,timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),stage:'Product Passport',record_id:passportId(batch),previous_hash:ledger.at(-1)?.hash||'GENESIS',payload_hash:await digest(x),evidence_hash:await digest(x.sup.map(s=>invoice(s)).join('')+meter(x.p)+meter(x.c)),selected_fields:['passport_id','linked_records','current_calculations','evidence_hash'],confidentiality:'Internal'};block.hash=await digest(block);ledger.push(block);showBlocks();$('passport-proof-status').textContent='Passport snapshot hash added. Existing Verify chain checks it.'}catch(e){$('passport-proof-status').textContent=e.message}finally{pending=false}}};
renderAll();
function passportFromHash(){let m=location.hash.match(/^#passport=([^&]+)/);if(m){let batch=decodeURIComponent(m[1]);if(passportContext(batch)){switchTab('customer');openPassport(batch)}}}
window.addEventListener('hashchange',passportFromHash);passportFromHash();
</script><style>
#passport-dialog,#dpp-document{width:min(1150px,94vw);max-height:90vh;overflow:auto;background:#102239;color:#edf4fa;border:1px solid #488f9f;border-radius:14px;padding:24px}dialog::backdrop{background:#000a}.dpp-section{border:1px solid #365369;border-radius:8px;margin:16px 0;padding:14px}.dpp-section>summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;color:#7de1cf;margin-bottom:12px}.dpp-table{overflow:auto}.dpp-warning{background:#493a21;border-left:4px solid #efbf62;padding:12px;margin:12px 0}.dpp-actions{margin:12px 0}#dpp-document{z-index:100}.dpp-section table{width:100%}@media print{body.dpp-printing>*{display:none!important}body.dpp-printing>#dpp-document{display:block!important;position:static;max-height:none;width:auto;color:#111;background:white;border:0;overflow:visible}body.dpp-printing .dpp-actions{display:none}body.dpp-printing table,body.dpp-printing td,body.dpp-printing th{color:#111!important;background:white!important}body.dpp-printing .dpp-warning{background:#fff!important;color:#111}}
</style><script>
// DPP dossier additions. Existing dataset and calculation engine remain unchanged.
const DPP_DATE='2026-10-16';
const dppName=b=>'DPP — '+(b.startsWith('FIN-')?'Finished Product Batch ':'PP Production Batch ')+b.slice(-1);
const dppEntity=id=>DB.entities.find(e=>e.entity_id===id)||{name:id,country:'India',city:'Not recorded'};
const dppPairs=o=>table(Object.entries(o).map(([field,value])=>({field,value})),['field','value']);
const dppSection=(title,body)=>'<details class="dpp-section" open><summary>'+esc(title)+'</summary>'+body+'</details>';
function dppIdentity(batch){let x=passportContext(batch),e=dppEntity(x.finished?x.c.customer_id:x.p.plant_id);return {Product:x.finished?'Reusable non-food PP storage article':'PP compound',Grade:'PP-COMP-'+batch.slice(-1),Manufacturer:e.name,'Manufacturing site':e.site_address||e.city,'Country of origin':e.country,'Batch number':batch,'Production date':x.finished?'2026-09-24':'2026-09-15',Quantity:fmt(x.finished?x.c.finished_product_kg:x.p.saleable_output_kg)+' kg','DPP ID':passportId(batch),'Parent batch':x.finished?x.p.output_batch_id:'See material origin', 'Formulation reference':'FORM-'+batch.slice(-1)+' / v1.0'};}
function dppDocs(batch){let x=passportContext(batch),grade='PP-COMP-'+batch.slice(-1),owner=x.finished?x.c.customer_id:x.p.plant_id;return ['SDS','TDS','CoA','Composition declaration','Recycled-content declaration','Test report'].map((type,i)=>({id:(i<2?grade:batch)+'-'+['SDS','TDS','COA','COMP','PCR','TEST'][i],type,version:'1.0',issued:'2026-09-25',issuer:dppEntity(i<2?x.p.plant_id:owner).name,applicable:i<2?grade+' / source '+x.p.output_batch_id+' and linked articles':batch,review_status:'Illustrative — not independently verified',review_due:i===4?'2026-10-01':'2026-12-31',owner:owner+' quality/EHS',evidence_type:'Embedded illustrative document',confidence:'Low',reason:'Demonstration content; no actual laboratory or third-party evidence',visibility:i===2||i===5?'Internal':'Public preview',batch}));}
function dppCompliance(batch){return ['SDS / hazard communication','GHS classification','REACH / ECHA-related obligations','CLP','UFI / PCN','OSHA hazard communication','Recycled-content claim'].map((requirement,i)=>({requirement,market:i===2||i===3||i===4?'EU — if supplied there':i===5?'USA — if supplied there':'India / destination market to confirm',applicability_reason:i===4?'Assess mixture classification and notification scope; not automatic for PP':i===6?'Substantiate declared physical recycled share':'Assess product form, composition, intended use and destination',status:'Pending assessment',supporting_document:i===6?batch+'-PCR':dppDocs(batch)[0].id,responsible_role:'EHS / regulatory reviewer',review_due:'2026-12-31'}));}
function dppDocumentBody(d){let x=passportContext(d.batch);let identity=dppIdentity(d.type==='SDS'||d.type==='TDS'?x.p.output_batch_id:d.batch);let body=dppPairs({Document:d.type,'Document reference':d.id,Version:d.version,'Issue date':d.issued,Issuer:d.issuer,'Applicable product/batch':d.applicable,'Review status':d.review_status,'Review due':d.review_due});
if(d.type==='SDS'){let sections=['Identification','Hazard identification','Composition / ingredients','First-aid measures','Fire-fighting measures','Accidental release measures','Handling and storage','Exposure controls / personal protection','Physical and chemical properties','Stability and reactivity','Toxicological information','Ecological information','Disposal considerations','Transport information','Regulatory information','Other information'];body+='<p>This is a document-structure example, not a safety assessment. Emergency contact: not provided. Obtain an approved supplier SDS before handling.</p>'+table(sections.map((s,i)=>({section:(i+1)+'. '+s,information:i===0?identity.Product+'; '+identity.Grade:i===2?'PP blend with recycled PP and additives; full formulation not assessed':'Pending qualified assessment — no operational guidance supplied'})),['section','information']);}
else if(d.type==='TDS'||d.type==='CoA'||d.type==='Test report'){body+='<p>Illustrative numerical values only. They are not measurements or validated product specifications.</p>'+table([{property:'Melt flow rate',method:'ISO 1133-1; 230 °C / 2.16 kg (illustrative)',unit:'g/10 min',specification:'10–14',result:'12.1'},{property:'Density',method:'ISO 1183-1 (illustrative)',unit:'g/cm³',specification:'0.89–0.93',result:'0.91'},{property:'Tensile strength',method:'ISO 527 (illustrative)',unit:'MPa',specification:'≥ 20',result:'24'}],['property','method','unit','specification','result'])+dppPairs({'Intended application':x.c.application,'Food contact approval':'Not claimed','Sample ID':'LAB-'+d.batch,'Test date':'2026-09-25','Laboratory':'Illustrative laboratory; no accreditation claimed','Release status':'Pending qualified approval','Reviewer / signature':'Not supplied'});}
else {body+=table(x.sup.map(s=>({material:s.material,source:s.supplier_id,input_kg:s.quantity_kg,input_share_pct:fmt(100*s.quantity_kg/x.p.input_kg),recycled_content_pct:s.physical_recycled_content_pct})),['material','source','input_kg','input_share_pct','recycled_content_pct'])+dppPairs({'Batch recycled share':x.c.physical_recycled_content_pct+'%','Calculation basis':'Proportional mixing and losses; no mass-balance credit','Certification':'No verified certificate supplied','Substance declaration':'Full additive / restricted-substance assessment pending'});}
return '<div class="dpp-warning">Illustrative document — not valid for operational use. No real certification or laboratory result is claimed.</div>'+body;}
function dppOpenDoc(batch,index){let d=dppDocs(batch)[index],m=$('dpp-document');if(!m){m=document.createElement('dialog');m.id='dpp-document';document.body.appendChild(m);}m.innerHTML='<div class="dpp-actions"><button class="action" id="dpp-doc-close">Close</button> <button class="action" id="dpp-doc-print">Print / Save as PDF</button></div><div id="dpp-print-content"><h2>'+esc(d.type)+' — '+esc(dppName(batch))+'</h2>'+dppDocumentBody(d)+'</div>';$('dpp-doc-close').onclick=()=>m.close();$('dpp-doc-print').onclick=()=>{document.body.classList.add('dpp-printing');window.print();document.body.classList.remove('dpp-printing');};if(!m.open)m.showModal();}
function dppRegister(batch,publicOnly=false){let ds=dppDocs(batch);return '<div class="dpp-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Document</th><th>Reference / version</th><th>Issuer / issued</th><th>Scope</th><th>Status / review due</th><th>Visibility</th><th>Open</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+ds.map((d,i)=>publicOnly&&d.visibility!=='Public preview'?'':'<tr><td>'+esc(d.type)+'</td><td>'+esc(d.id)+' / '+d.version+'</td><td>'+esc(d.issuer)+'<br>'+d.issued+'</td><td>'+esc(d.applicable)+'</td><td>'+esc(d.review_status)+'<br>'+d.review_due+'</td><td>'+d.visibility+'</td><td><button class="action" data-dpp-doc="'+i+'" data-batch="'+batch+'">View document</button></td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></div>';}
const dppBasePassportHtml=passportHtml;
passportHtml=function(batch,publicOnly=false){let x=passportContext(batch);if(!x)return '<p>DPP not found.</p>';let {p,c,sup,recovery}=x,old=dppBasePassportHtml(batch);let journey=old.slice(old.indexOf('<h3>Shipment history</h3>'),old.indexOf('<details><summary>Sustainability figures'));
let foot=x.finished?c.selected_finished_product_emissions_kgco2e:p.selected_gate_emissions_kgco2e,qty=x.finished?c.finished_product_kg:p.saleable_output_kg;
return '<h2>Digital Product Passport (DPP)</h2><h3>'+esc(dppName(batch))+'</h3><div class="dpp-actions"><button class="action" data-dpp-mode="'+(publicOnly?'internal':'public')+'" data-batch="'+batch+'">'+(publicOnly?'View full dossier':'Preview customer view')+'</button></div><p class="muted">'+(publicOnly?'Public-view demonstration: illustrative documents have not been approved for publication.':'Internal dossier view · illustrative records.')+' Visibility controls in this HTML are a display preview, not secure access control.</p>'+dppSection('1 · Product & Batch Identity',dppPairs(dppIdentity(batch))+'<img width="160" height="160" alt="Batch DPP QR" src="'+PASSPORT_QR[batch]+'"><p>Scan to view this batch’s DPP. Updated HTML must be published at the linked website.</p><a class="source-link" href="https://nimisha-raina.github.io/ISB/#passport='+batch+'">Open batch DPP link</a>'+(x.finished?'<p>'+passportButton(p.output_batch_id,'View source PP DPP')+'</p>':''))+dppSection('2 · Material Composition',table(sup.map(s=>({material:s.material,input_kg:s.quantity_kg,blend_pct:fmt(s.quantity_kg/p.input_kg*100),recycled_pct:s.physical_recycled_content_pct})),['material','input_kg','blend_pct','recycled_pct'])+'<p>Physical recycled share: '+c.physical_recycled_content_pct+'%. Full additive chemistry and substance declarations: pending assessment.</p>')+dppSection('3 · Safety & Technical Documents',dppRegister(batch,publicOnly)+'<p>Certification register: no verified third-party certificate supplied. SDS/TDS are grade-level references; CoA and test records identify a batch.</p>')+dppSection('4 · Compliance',table(dppCompliance(batch),['requirement','market','applicability_reason','status','supporting_document','responsible_role','review_due'])+'<p>Applicability has not been legally assessed. ECHA is an authority, UFI an identifier and PCN a notification process; these are not interchangeable certificates.</p>')+dppSection('5 · Sustainability',kpis([['Recorded footprint',fmt(foot)+' kg CO₂e'],['Intensity',fmt(foot/qty,4)+' kg CO₂e/kg'],['Recycled share',c.physical_recycled_content_pct+'%'],['Evidence status','Illustrative']])+dppPairs({'Footprint boundary':'Selected cradle-to-'+(x.finished?'converter':'plant')+'-gate; excludes use and final end-of-life','Allocation':'Selected burdens assigned to output; proportional downstream allocation; no avoided-emission credit','Plant water withdrawal':p.water_withdrawal_m3+' m³','Plant water consumption':p.water_consumption_m3+' m³','Plant water discharge':p.water_discharge_m3+' m³','Product water footprint':'Not assessed; displayed site water is not a full product water footprint'})+table(DB.emission_factors,['factor_id','value','unit','boundary','source']))+dppSection('6 · Journey & Evidence',publicOnly?'<p>PP production batch '+p.output_batch_id+' → distributor → converter → finished batch '+c.finished_product_batch_id+'. Commercial evidence omitted from this display preview.</p>':journey+dppEvidence(batch))+dppSection('7 · End-of-Life','<p><b>Finished-product use and final disposal: Not yet recorded.</b> Manufacturing scrap recovery is separate from recovery of sold products.</p><p>Illustrative separation guidance: identify the PP article, separate removable non-PP parts where feasible, and ask the receiving recycler about acceptance and contamination restrictions. Product-specific instructions require validation; recyclability is not guaranteed. Do not infer food-contact suitability.</p>'+table(recovery,['source_batch_id','collection_date','recycler_id','received_kg','recovered_kg','reject_kg','certificate_id'])+'<p>Repair / spare parts: not assessed for this article. Durability and actual consumer collection: not recorded.</p>');};
function dppEvidence(batch){return table(dppDocs(batch),['id','owner','evidence_type','review_status','issued','review_due','confidence','reason'])+'<p>Missing evidence: approved SDS/TDS, genuine CoA/test reports, verified recycled-content certificate and final-use records. Confidence does not establish compliance.</p>';}
const dppOldBind=bindPassports;
bindPassports=function(host){dppOldBind(host);host.querySelectorAll('[data-dpp-doc]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>dppOpenDoc(b.dataset.batch,Number(b.dataset.dppDoc)));host.querySelectorAll('[data-dpp-mode]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openPassport(b.dataset.batch,b.dataset.dppMode==='public'));};
openPassport=function(batch,publicOnly=false){let m=$('passport-dialog');if(!m){m=document.createElement('dialog');m.id='passport-dialog';document.body.appendChild(m);}m.innerHTML='<button class="action" id="passport-close" style="float:right">Close</button>'+passportHtml(batch,publicOnly);bindPassports(m);$('passport-close').onclick=()=>m.close();if(!m.open)m.showModal();};
passportButton=(batch,label)=>'<button class="action" data-passport="'+esc(batch)+'">'+esc(label||'View DPP')+'</button>';
function dppTabFields(t,r,b){let x=passportContext(b);let common={'Linked DPP':dppName(b)};if(t==='supplier')return {...common,'Supplied grade':r.grade,'Supplier batch':r.supplier_batch_id,'Supplier SDS / TDS':r.sds_id+' / TDS-'+r.supplier_id,'Supplier CoA':r.coa_id,'Composition declaration':'Pending supplier approval','Recycled-content certificate':r.certificate_id||'Not supplied','Substance declaration':'Pending assessment','Receiving batch':x.p.output_batch_id};if(t==='transport')return {...common,'Transport document':r.evidence_reference,'Dispatch / receipt':r.dispatch_date+' / '+r.receipt_date,'Received quantity':r.quantity_received_kg+' kg','Handling requirements':'Approved SDS / carrier instructions required','Transport classification':'Pending qualified assessment','Incident record':r.spill_count+' spills recorded in illustrative dataset'};if(t==='plant')return {...common,'Production date':'2026-09-15','Formulation':'FORM-'+b.slice(-1)+' / v1.0','Quality results':'View illustrative batch CoA / test report','Release approval':'Pending qualified approval','SDS / TDS version':'1.0 — illustrative'};if(t==='distributor')return {...common,'Received / dispatched':r.received_kg+' / '+r.dispatched_kg+' kg','Remaining stock':r.closing_stock_kg+' kg','Destination':r.customer_id,'Storage conditions':'Approved grade-specific guidance pending','Document handover':'References linked; recipient acknowledgement pending','Split / repack':'No repacking recorded; dispatch draws from parent '+r.batch_id,'Child-lot ID':'Not assigned — no new repacked lot'};if(t==='customer')return {...common,'Parent PP batch':r.input_batch_id,'Application':r.application,'Safety / use information':'Grade-specific review pending; no food-contact claim','Product handling':'See document register; illustrative guidance only'};return {...common,'Collection date':r.collection_date,'Recycler':r.recycler_id,'Source category':'Manufacturing / conversion scrap','Recovery certificate':r.certificate_id,'Certificate verification':'Not verified','Final-product collection':'Not yet recorded'};}
const dppOldEntity=renderEntity;
renderEntity=function(t){dppOldEntity(t);let r=DB[cfg[t][0]].find(x=>x[cfg[t][1]]===selection[t]),b=linkedBatch(t,r);if(!b)return;let host=$(t).querySelector('.passport-card');if(host){host.innerHTML='<h3>'+esc(dppName(b))+'</h3>'+dppPairs(dppTabFields(t,r,b))+passportButton(b);bindPassports(host);}};
const dppOldConfidence=renderConfidence;
renderConfidence=function(){dppOldConfidence();let box=document.createElement('div');box.className='box';box.innerHTML='<h3>DPP Document Confidence & Review</h3><p>Existing activity confidence and document confidence are separate. Illustrative documents are Low confidence, irrespective of the activity scenario score.</p>'+rows('customers').map(c=>'<h4>'+dppName(c.finished_product_batch_id)+'</h4>'+dppEvidence(c.finished_product_batch_id)).join('');$('dependency').querySelector('.card').appendChild(box);};
const dppOldESG=renderESG;
renderESG=function(){dppOldESG();let cs=rows('customers'),ds=cs.flatMap(c=>dppDocs(c.finished_product_batch_id)),over=ds.filter(d=>d.review_due<DPP_DATE).length;let host=$('holistic').querySelector('.passport-card');if(host)host.innerHTML='<h3>DPP Coverage & Data Confidence</h3>'+kpis([['Finished-batch DPPs',cs.length+' / '+cs.length],['Illustrative document slots',ds.length+' / '+ds.length],['Verified documents','0 / '+ds.length],['Overdue document reviews',over]])+'<p>Review as of '+DPP_DATE+'. Document coverage counts illustrative records, not approved evidence. Missing information is not scored as poor environmental performance.</p>'+table(cs.map(c=>({DPP:dppName(c.finished_product_batch_id),identity_fields:Object.keys(dppIdentity(c.finished_product_batch_id)).length+' / '+Object.keys(dppIdentity(c.finished_product_batch_id)).length+' populated',dossier_sections:'7 / 7 displayed',documents:'6 / 6 illustrative; 0 verified',compliance:'Pending assessment',document_confidence:'Low',activity_confidence:c.confidence,final_end_of_life:'0% recorded'})),['DPP','identity_fields','dossier_sections','documents','compliance','document_confidence','activity_confidence','final_end_of_life']);};
const dppOldChain=renderChain;
renderChain=function(){dppOldChain();let h=$('blockchain').querySelector('.passport-card');if(!h)return;h.innerHTML='<h3>DPP Dossier Integrity Snapshot</h3><select id="passport-proof-batch">'+rows('customers').flatMap(c=>[c.input_batch_id,c.finished_product_batch_id]).map(b=>'<option value="'+b+'">'+dppName(b)+'</option>').join('')+'</select> <button class="action" id="passport-proof-add">Add DPP snapshot hash</button><p>Includes current linked records, dossier fields and document content/version references. Integrity checks do not establish authenticity, compliance or truth. This ledger remains a local, non-persistent demonstration.</p><div id="passport-proof-status"></div>';$('passport-proof-add').onclick=async()=>{if(pending)return;pending=true;try{let batch=$('passport-proof-batch').value;let dossier={identity:dppIdentity(batch),records:passportContext(batch),documents:dppDocs(batch),compliance:dppCompliance(batch)};let block={index:ledger.length,timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),stage:'DPP dossier',record_id:passportId(batch),previous_hash:ledger.at(-1)?.hash||'GENESIS',payload_hash:await digest(dossier),evidence_hash:await digest(dppDocs(batch).map(d=>dppDocumentBody(d))),selected_fields:['identity','linked_records','document_versions','document_content','compliance_assessments'],confidentiality:'Internal'};block.hash=await digest(block);ledger.push(block);showBlocks();$('passport-proof-status').textContent='DPP dossier snapshot added. Use Verify chain to check consistency.';}catch(e){$('passport-proof-status').textContent=e.message;}finally{pending=false;}};};
// QR links open the customer-facing preview; full embedded data remain accessible in this static demo.
passportFromHash=function(){let m=location.hash.match(/^#passport=([^&]+)/);if(m){let b=decodeURIComponent(m[1]);if(passportContext(b)){switchTab('customer');openPassport(b,true);}}};
window.addEventListener('hashchange',passportFromHash);
renderAll();passportFromHash();

</script></body></html>
